L. 170-1 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf This title defines the conditions under which controls are carried out on installations, works, works, operations, objects, devices and activities governed by this code as well as the sanctions applicable in the event of failure or violation of the requirements provided for by this code. this code. The specific provisions relating to controls and sanctions appearing in the other titles of this book and in the other books of this code deviate from these common provisions or supplement them. Chapter I: Administrative controls and administrative police measures Section 1: Administrative controls
L. 171-1 LAW n°2023-54 of February 2, 2023 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I. # The officials and agents responsible for the controls provided for in article
L. 170-1 have access to: 1° To premises hosting installations, structures, works, developments, operations, objects, devices and activities subject to the provisions of this code, excluding premises to residential use. They may enter these places between 8 a.m. and 8 p.m. and, outside these hours, when they are open to the public or when production, manufacturing, processing, use, packaging, storage, deposit, transport or marketing mentioned in this code; 2° In other places, in particular enclosures, at any time, where activities subject to the provisions of this code are carried out or are likely to be carried out; 3° To vehicles, ships, boats, boats and aircraft used for the possession, transport, conservation or marketing of animals, plants or any other product likely to constitute a breach of the requirements of this code. II. # Officials and agents responsible for inspections may only have access to homes and the part of the premises used for residential purposes in the presence of the occupant and with their consent.
L. 171-2 Ordinance no. 2019-964 of September 18, 2019 - art. 35 (VD) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I. # When access to the places mentioned in 1° and 3° of I of the article
L. 171-1 is refused to agents if the person qualified to authorize access cannot be reached or when the access conditions set out in II of the same article are not met, visits may be authorized by order of the judge of freedoms and detention of the judicial court in whose jurisdiction the places or premises to be visited are located. The order includes the address of the places to be visited, the name and status of the agent(s) authorized to carry out the inspection operations as well as the times at which they are authorized to appear. The order is enforceable on the sole basis of the minute. II. # The order is notified on site at the time of the visit to the occupant of the premises or his representative, who receives a full copy against receipt or signature in the visit report. In the absence of the occupant of the premises or his representative, the order is notified, after the visit, by registered letter with request p.102 Environmental Code https://juricaf.org/recherche/article%20L .%20171-2%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VII: Common provisions relating to controls and sanctions for notice of receipt . Notification is deemed to have been made on the date of receipt appearing on the notice. In the absence of receipt, the order is served by means of a bailiff. The notification act includes mention of the avenues and deadlines for appealing against the order authorizing the visit and against the conduct of the visit operations. It also mentions that the judge who authorized the visit may receive a request to suspend or stop this visit. III. # The visit is carried out under the authority and control of the judge of freedoms and detention who authorized it. The liberty and detention judge may, if he considers it useful, visit the premises during the intervention. At any time, he can decide to suspend or stop the visit. Referral to the judge of freedoms and detention for the purpose of suspending or stopping visit operations has no suspensive effect. IV. # The visit is carried out in the presence of the occupant of the premises or his representative, who may be assisted by counsel of his choice. In the absence of the occupant of the premises, the agents responsible for the visit can only carry out the visit in the presence of two witnesses who are not aces under their authority. A report relating the modalities and progress of the operation and recording the findings made is drawn up immediately by the agents who carried out the visit. The report is signed by these agents and by the occupant of the premises or, where applicable, his representative and the witnesses. In the event of refusal to sign, mention will be made in the minutes. The original of the report is, as soon as it has been drawn up, sent to the judge who authorized the visit. A copy of this same document is given or sent by registered letter with acknowledgment of receipt to the occupant of the premises or his representative. The report mentions the deadline and the means of appeal. V. # The order authorizing the visit may be appealed before the first president of the court of appeal following the rules provided for by the code of civil procedure. The parties are not required to appoint a lawyer. This appeal is made by declaration delivered or sent by registered letter to the court registry within fifteen days. This period runs from notification of the order. This appeal is not suspensive. The registry of the judicial court immediately transmits the case file to the registry of the court of appeal where the parties can consult it. The order of the first president of the court of appeal is subject to an appeal in cassation, according to the rules provided for by the code of civil procedure. The time limit for an appeal to the Court of Cassation is fifteen days. VI. # The first president of the court of appeal hears appeals against the conduct of visit operations authorized by the judge of freedoms and detention according to the rules provided for by the code of civil procedure. The parties are not required to appoint a lawyer. The appeal is made by declaration delivered or sent by registered mail to the court registry within fifteen days. This period starts from the delivery or receipt of the visit report. This appeal is not suspensive. The order of the first president of the court of appeal is subject to an appeal in cassation according to the rules provided for by the code of civil procedure. The time limit for an appeal to the Court of Cassation is fifteen days. VII. # This article is reproduced in the act of notification of the order of the judge of freedoms and detention authorizing the visit e.
L. 171-3 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Officials and agents responsible for controls can request and take a copy of documents relating to the subject of the control, whatever their medium and in whatever hands they are in, and which are necessary for the accomplishment of their mission. They can only take the original documents after having established a list which is countersigned by their holder. The original documents are returned within one month after the inspection. When the documents are in computerized form, officials and agents have access to the software and this data. They may request the transcription of this data by any appropriate processing into documents directly usable for the purposes of control. p.103 Environmental Code http://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichTexte.do?cidTexte=JORFTEXT000025134953 https://legifrance.gouv.fr/codes/article_lc/LEGIARTI000025136604/2024-05-14 https:// www.courdecassation.fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20171-3%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=cc&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://www.courdecassation.fr/recherche -judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20171-3%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=ca&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://legifrance.gouv.fr/search/cetat?tab_selection=cetat&searchField= ALL&query=%22article%20L171-3%22%20du%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&page=1&init=true&dateDecision= https://juricaf.org/recherche/article%20L.%20171-3%20% 22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VII: Common provisions relating to controls and sanctions
L. 171-3 -1 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 22 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.-Officials and agents responsible for controls may take or have samples taken for analysis or testing. These samples are placed under seal. Within the perimeter of an installation, the person in charge present or, failing that, his representative is informed that he can witness the sampling. The absence of the person responsible or his representative does not prevent the collection. II.-The samples are taken at least in duplicate and sent to an analysis laboratory. A copy is kept by the official or agent responsible for control for the purposes of a second opinion. The person subject to the inspection, or the person designated to represent them, is informed that they can have the preserved copy analyzed at their own expense. It makes its decision known within five days following the date on which the results of the laboratory analysis were brought to its attention. After this period, the copy may be eliminated. If no second opinion has been requested, the second sample is destroyed after a period of two months from the date of collection.
L. 171-4 Ordinance No. 2016-128 of February 10, 2016 - art. 34 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Officials and agents responsible for controls may collect, upon summons or on site, information and justifications specific to the accomplishment of their mission.
L. 171-5 -1 Ordinance No. 2016-128 of February 10, 2016 - art. 34 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The civil servants and agents responsible for inspections may be assisted, during inspections, by experts designated by the administrative authority. These experts are bound by professional secrecy in accordance with the provisions of articles 226-13 and 226-14 of the penal code.
L. 171-5 -2 LAW n°2021-1104 of August 22, 2021 - art. 282 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf In order to ensure the exercise of their administrative police missions and the detection of offenses punishable by administrative sanctions provided for respectively in section 2 of this chapter and in subsection 3 of section 2 of chapter II of title IV of book I of the energy code, the agents responsible for the controls provided for in article
L. 170-1 of this code and the agents mentioned in article
L. 142-21 of the Energy Code may, on occasion and within the framework of these controls relating to the installations mentioned in article
L. 511-1 of this code and on hydraulic works subject to articles L. 214-1
L. 214-2 or authorized in application of Title I of Book V of the Energy Code or granted under the same code, proceed, by means of cameras and sensors installed on aircraft circulating without anyone on board and operated by a remote pilot, the capture, recording and transmission of images as well as physical or chemical data. The only recipients of recorded images and data are agents, duly trained and authorized, who need to know them to carry out these missions. The occupant of the premises or his representative who attends the inspection is informed of the use of an aircraft traveling without anyone on board. When the visit to the premises is authorized by an order from the judge of freedoms and detention, on the basis of article
L. 171-2 of this code or article
L. 142-23 of the Energy Code, the latter is informed in advance of the intention to use such an aircraft. The use of aircraft mentioned in the first paragraph of this article is only possible in the following cases: 1° The technical or material conditions of the control make ground surveys difficult or reduce their reliability compared to the use of aircraft; 2° The safety of control agents is better guaranteed by an aerial survey; 3° Land surveys would result in excessive additional administrative costs. When implemented in public spaces, the operations mentioned in the first paragraph are carried out in such a way that they neither visualize images of the interior of homes nor, specifically, those of https://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichCodeArticle.do?cidTexte=LEGITEXT000023983208&idArticle=LEGIARTI000023986010&dateTexte=&categorieLien=cid Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VII: Common provisions relating to controls and sanctions Cameras mentioned in the same first paragraph are only used on sites or around sites whose surveillance is made necessary for the accomplishment of the missions and the prosecution of the offenses mentioned in the first paragraph. Registration is not permanent and is only made possible in cases exhaustively listed by the decree provided for in the last paragraph, which are the subject of a customary doctrine disseminated by the minister responsible for the environment. The cameras are equipped with technical devices to guarantee the integrity of the data thus collected. The analysis of images from cameras using automated facial recognition devices, as well as the automated interconnections, reconciliations or linking of personal data collected with other processing of personal data, is prohibited. The new technologies thus implemented have no impact on the exercise of the rights of the persons concerned provided for by Law No. 78-17 of January 6, 1978 relating to data processing, files and freedoms. When the recordings linked to these operations carried out in the public space give rise to an administrative procedure, they are kept until the expiry of the time limits for appealing against the acts taken within the framework of this procedure and, in the event of litigation , until the closure of legal proceedings and the exhaustion of avenues of appeal. When they do not give rise to an administrative procedure, they are deleted after six months, and after thirty days when they contain personal data. The terms of application of this article are specified by a decree in the Council of State, taken after consulting the National Commission for Information Technology and Liberties. L. 171-8 for the purposes of obtaining the execution of this decision. Section 2: Operations to search for and report offenses
L. 172-4 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Environmental inspectors mentioned in the article
L. 172-1 and other civil servants and agents of the State, local authorities and their public establishments authorized under the special policies of this code to investigate and report infringements of the provisions of this code and the texts taken to its application exercise their powers under the conditions provided for in this section. When they are authorized to investigate and report infringements of other legislative provisions, they exercise their powers under these same conditions. Judicial police officers, judicial police officers and deputy judicial police officers mentioned in articles 16, 20 and 21 of the code of criminal procedure are authorized to investigate and report offenses against this code under the conditions defined by the others. books of this code. They carry out these missions within the limits and according to the terms set by the code of criminal procedure.
L. 172-5 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The officials and agents mentioned in the article
L. 172-4 search for and note the offenses provided for by this code wherever they are committed. However, they are required to inform the public prosecutor, who may object, before accessing: 1° To establishments, professional premises and installations in which production, manufacturing, processing activities are carried out, use, packaging, storage, deposit, transport or marketing. They cannot enter these places before 6 a.m. and after 9 p.m. Outside of these hours, they access it when the premises are open to the public or when one of the activities provided for above is in progress; 2° To vehicles, ships, boats, boats and professional aircraft used for the possession, transport, conservation or marketing of animals, plants or any other product likely to be the subject of an offense provided for by the this code. Visits to homes and premises containing residential areas cannot be started before 6 a.m. and after 9 p.m., with the consent of the occupant or, failing that, in the presence of a judicial police officer. acting in accordance with the provisions of the code of criminal procedure relating to home visits, searches and seizures of evidence. This assent must be the subject of a written declaration e in the hand of the person concerned or, if he cannot write, this is mentioned in the minutes, as well as his assent. L. 172-6 Ordinance No. 2019-964 of September 18, 2019 - art. 35 (VD) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When they search for animals, plants or minerals, or their parts and products, taken in violation of the provisions of Chapter I of Title III of Book III, Chapters I and II of Title I and Titles II and III of book IV, the officials and agents mentioned in article
L. 172-4 can follow them to all the places where they have been transported. However, they may only enter homes or premises containing residential areas with the consent of the occupant expressed under the conditions provided for in article
L. 172-5 or, failing that, with the authorization of the judge of freedoms and detention of the judicial court in whose jurisdiction the places to be visited are located.
L. 172-7 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When an official or agent mentioned in article
L. 172-4 intends to draw up a report against a person who refuses or is unable to prove their identity, article 78-3 of the code of criminal procedure is applied. During the time necessary for the information and the decision of the judicial police officer, the alleged perpetrator of the offense is required to remain at the disposal of the investigating officer.
L. 172-8 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The officials and agents mentioned in the article
L. 172-4 may collect, upon summons or on site, statements from any person likely to provide useful elements for their findings. They draw up a report. The people heard read it themselves, can have their observations recorded and add their signature. If they declare that they cannot read, it will be read to them by the agent prior to signing. In the event of refusal to sign the report, mention will be made on it. In accordance with article 28 of the code of criminal procedure, article 61-1 of the same code is applicable when a person is interviewed about whom there are plausible reasons to suspect that he has committed or attempted to commit an offense. The fact, without legitimate reason, of not complying with the summons to the hearing constitutes the offense of obstacle to duties provided for in article L. 173-4
L. 172-10 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The officials and agents mentioned in the article
L. 172-4 may, in the exercise of their functions, be requested by the public prosecutor, the investigating judge and judicial police officers. They are authorized to directly request the police to investigate or report violations of the provisions of this code and the texts adopted for its application. The environmental inspectors mentioned in the article
L. 172-1 assigned to the French Biodiversity Office may receive letters rogatory from the investigating judge.
L. 172-11 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The officials and agents mentioned in the article
L. 172-4 may request the communication, take a copy or seize documents of any nature which relate to the subject of the control, whatever their medium and in whatever hands they are found, and which are necessary for the accomplishment of their mission without the obligation of professional secrecy being imposed on them, without legitimate reason. When the documents are in computerized form, they have access to software and data; they can request transcription, on site and immediately, by any appropriate processing into documents that can be relating to controls and sanctions They can also consult any document necessary for the accomplishment of their mission to public administrations, establishments and organizations placed under the control of the State and local authorities. They may also carry out the requisitions provided for in articles 77-1, 77-1-1 and 77-1-2 of the code of criminal procedure, under the same conditions as those provided for judicial police officers. e.
L. 172-11 -1 LAW n°2016-1087 of August 8, 2016 - art. 130 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf For the sole purpose of recording the offenses provided for in articles L. 415-3
L. 415-6 when these are committed using electronic means of communication, environmental inspectors authorized under conditions specified by order of the Ministers of Justice and responsible for ecology may, without being criminally liable responsible for these acts: 1° Participate under a pseudonym in electronic exchanges; 2° Be in contact by this means with people likely to be the perpetrators of these offenses; 3° Acquire products or substances. Under penalty of nullity, these acts cannot have the effect of inciting others to commit an offense.
L. 172-12 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The officials and agents mentioned in the article
L. 172-4 may: 1° Proceed with the seizure of the object or direct or indirect product of the offense, including animals, plants and minerals, their parts or products, as well as weapons and ammunition, objects, instruments and devices used to commit the offense or intended for it; 2° Proceed with the seizure of boats, automobiles and other vehicles used by the perpetrators of an offense to commit the offense, to go to the place where the offense was committed or to leave it, or to transport the object of the offense. The seizure is recorded in a report drawn up by them. These provisions do not apply when the objects or devices have been the subject of a deposit pursuant to article
L. 172-15. The costs of transport, maintenance and custody of the seized objects are borne by the offender.
L. 172-13 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.- When they seized them, the officials and agents mentioned in article
L. 172-4 may carry out or have carried out the destruction of dead or non-viable plants and animals. II.-Upon authorization from the public prosecutor issued by any means, the civil servants and agents mentioned in article
L. 172-4 may proceed or arrange for the placement of viable animals and plants seized in a storage location provided for this purpose. When their conservation is no longer necessary for the manifestation of the truth, the officials and agents mentioned in the same article
L. 172-4 may proceed or cause to be carried out: 1° The return of non-domestic or untamed animals and uncultivated plants, seized in a viable state, to the natural environment from which they were taken or to an environment compatible with their biological requirements; 2° The destruction of animals likely to cause damage; 3° When the animal does not fall under 1° and 2° of this II, the application of the provisions provided for in Article 99-1 of the Code of Criminal Procedure; 4° With authorization from the public prosecutor, the destruction of property mentioned in the fourth paragraph of article 41-5 of the same code which does not fall under 1°, 2° and 3° of this II, under the conditions provided for in the penultimate paragraph of article 41-5 of the code of criminal procedure. III.-The placement, return to the natural environment and destruction are recorded in the report. p.111 Environmental Code L. 172-14 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I. # The officials and agents mentioned in the article
L. 172-4 may take or have samples taken for analysis or testing. These samples are placed under seal. Within the perimeter of an installation, the person in charge present or, failing that, his representative is informed that he can witness the sampling. The absence of the person responsible does not prevent the collection. II. # The samples are taken at least in duplicate and sent to an analysis laboratory. A copy is kept for the purposes of a second opinion. The person accused or their representative is informed that they can have the preserved copy analyzed at their own expense. It makes its decision known within five days following the date on which the results of the laboratory analysis were brought to its attention. After this period, the copy may be eliminated. When the author of the facts was not identified at the time of the sample, the investigating officer assesses whether a second analysis is necessary to reveal the truth. Otherwise, the copy kept for the purposes of a second opinion is eliminated within the time limit set by the public prosecutor.
L. 172-15 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When additional investigations are necessary, the judge of freedoms and detention in whose jurisdiction objects or devices suspected of being non-compliant with the provisions of this code and the texts taken for its application are held may, at the request of the public prosecutor, authorize the officials and agents mentioned in article
L. 172-4 to proceed with their recording during these investigations. The consignment measure, the duration of which cannot exceed fifteen days, may, in the event of particular difficulty, be renewed by reasoned order. The judge of freedoms and detention may order the release of the consignment order at any time. This release is legal in all cases where the authorized agents have noted the conformity of the objects or devices recorded with the provisions of this code and the texts taken for its application or their compliance with these provisions. Returned items are left in the custody of their holder. In the event of non-compliance, any costs will be borne by the perpetrator.
L. 172-16 LAW n°2019-773 of July 24, 2019 - art. 4 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Violations of the provisions of this code and the texts taken for its application are noted by reports which are authentic until proven otherwise. The reports are sent within five days following their closing to the public prosecutor. A copy of the report is sent, within the same period, to the competent administrative authority. Unless otherwise instructed by the public prosecutor, a copy of the report establishing the offense is also sent to the offender, when it is known, within a time limit set by decree of the Council of State. With authorization from the public prosecutor, the names and surnames of the persons appearing in the copies of this report, with the exception of those of the offender, may be canceled when these mentions are likely to endanger life or limb. physical integrity of these people or those of their loved ones. Circulars and Instructions > Technical note of January 16, 2018 relating to the commissioning and exercise of judicial police functions of agents of nature reserves and coastal guards.

L. 173-4 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Obstructing the functions exercised by civil servants and agents authorized to carry out administrative control missions or research and detection of offenses in application of this code is punishable by six months' imprisonment and 15,000 euros fine. fine.
L. 173-5 LAW n°2021-1104 of August 22, 2021 - art. 290 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf In the event of conviction of a natural or legal person for an offense provided for in this code, the court may: 1° When the operation, work, activity, use of a work or installation at the origin of the offense are subject to authorization, registration, declaration, approval or certification, decide to stop or suspend them for a period which cannot exceed one year; 2° Order, within a period it determines, measures intended to restore the places affected by the incriminated acts or to repair the damage caused to the environment. The injunction may be accompanied by a daily penalty of up to €3,000, for a period of up to one year, as well as provisional enforcement. The court may decide that these measures will be executed automatically at the operator's expense. In this case, he may order the deposit by the operator into the hands of a public accountant of a sum corresponding to the amount of the work to be carried out. The measures provided for in this article may be ordered according to the same terms in the event of conviction for an offense provided for in this code according to the simplified procedure of the penal order provided for in articles 495 to 495-6 of the code of criminal procedure or according to the procedure of the appearance on prior recognition of guilt provided for in articles 495-7 to 495-16 of the same code.
L. 173-6 Ordinance No. 2012-34 of January 11, 2012 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When the court orders a suspension measure, and for the duration of this suspension, the operator is required to ensure that its staff pay the salaries, allowances and remuneration of all kinds to which they were entitled until then.

relating to controls and sanctions 1° The display and dissemination of the decision pronounced, under the conditions provided for in article 131-35 of the penal code; 2° The confiscation of the thing L. 173-8 LAW n°2021-1104 of August 22, 2021 - art. 280 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Legal entities recognized as criminally liable under the conditions provided for in article 121-2 of the penal code for the tort offenses provided for in this code incur, in addition to the fine under the conditions set out in article 131-38 of the penal code, the penalties provided for in 1°, 3°, 4°, 5°, 6°, 8°, 9° and 12° of article 131-39 of the same code as well as that provided for in 2° of this same article, which, if it is pronounced, applies to the activity in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of which the offense was committed.
L. 173-9 LAW no. 2020-1672 of December 24, 2020 - art. 23 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The provisions of articles 132-66 to 132-70 of the penal code on adjournment with injunction are applicable to natural persons and legal entities in the event of conviction for an offense provided for in this code. By way of derogation from article 132-69 of the penal code, when application of 2° of article
L. 173-5 of this code, the decision on the sentence takes place no later than two years after the postponement decision. The court may attach a penalty of up to 3,000 euros per day of delay to the injunction.

L. 173-12 LAW n°2016-1087 of August 8, 2016 - art. 135 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I. - The administrative authority may, as long as public action has not been initiated, compromise with natural persons and legal entities on the prosecution of contraventions and offenses provided for and punished by this code, with the exception offenses punishable by more than two years of imprisonment. The transaction proposed by the administration and accepted by the offender must be approved by the public prosecutor. II. - This option is not applicable to contraventions for which public action is extinguished by the payment of a fixed fine in application of article 529 of the code of criminal procedure. III. - The proposed transaction is determined based on the circumstances and seriousness of the offense, the personality of its perpetrator as well as his resources and charges. p.115 Environmental https://juricaf.org/recherche/article%20L.%20173-12%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC https://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichCodeArticle. do?cidTexte=LEGITEXT000006071154&idArticle=LEGIARTI000006576826&dateTexte=&categorieLien=cid Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VII: Common provisions relating to controls and sanctions It specifies the transactional fine that the author of the offense will have to pay, of which the amount cannot exceed one third of the amount of the fine incurred, as well as, where applicable, the obligations which will be imposed on it, tending to put an end to the infringement, to avoid its renewal, to repair the damage or to bring it into compliance places. It also sets the deadlines for payment and, where applicable, execution of obligations. IV. - Acts tending to the implementation or execution of the transaction interrupt the limitation period for public action. The public action is terminated when the author of the offense has fulfilled within the allotted time all the obligations resulting for him from the acceptance of the transaction. V. - The terms of application of this article are set by decree in the Council of Eta t.

L. 161-1 of the mining code depending on the case. II.-Environmental authorization can only be granted if the measures it includes also ensure: 1° Compliance with the provisions of articles L. 229-5

L. 332-2 as well as, where applicable, the implementation of the regulation or obligation mentioned in article

L. 341-1 as well as those mentioned in the classification decision, when the environmental authorization takes the place of the special authorization provided for by articles L. 341-7

L. 411-2, issuing exemptions from the prohibitions enacted for the conservation of sites of geological interest, natural habitats, non-domestic animal or non-cultivated plant species and their habitats, when environmental authorization takes place of this exemption; 5° Compliance with the conservation objectives of the Natura 2000 site, when the environmental authorization takes the place of the absence of opposition mentioned in VI of the article

L. 181-4 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Projects subject to environmental authorization pursuant to article
L. 181-1 remain subject, subject to the provisions of this title: 1° To the provisions of Title I of Book II for projects falling under 1° of Article
L. 181-1, of Title I of Book V for those falling under 2° of the same article, or of Titles II, IV, V and VI of Book I of the mining code and of Article
L. 131-1 of the same code for those falling under 3° of the same article; 2° To legislation specific to authorizations, registrations, declarations, absence of opposition, approvals and approvals for which environmental authorization takes place when they are required and which are listed in article
L. 181-2, as well as, where applicable, other specific legislative and regulatory provisions which govern them. Section 2: Request for authorization
L. 181-5 LAW n°2023-175 of March 10, 2023 - art. 12 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The holder of a project subject to environmental authorization, in the case where the project is also subject to a case-by-case examination under the environmental assessment, enters, before submitting the environmental authorization application, the the authority mentioned in IV of the article
L. 122-1, in order to determine whether the project must be subject to environmental assessment. In addition, the holder of a project subject to environmental authorization can: 1° Request information enabling him to prepare his project and the file for his authorization request from the competent administrative authority. The answers provided by the latter depend on the state of the project and do not prejudge either the content of the file which will ultimately be necessary for the examination of the authorization request nor the decision which will be taken at the end of it. -this ; 2° (Repealed); 3° (Repealed); 4° If the project is subject to environmental assessment, ask the competent authority for an opinion on the scope and degree of precision of the information to be provided in the impact study provided for in Article L. 122-1
L. 181-7 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When a petitioner plans to carry out his project, within the meaning of article
L. 122-1, in several tranches, simultaneous or successive, it may request separate environmental authorizations for those of the tranches which require them. This possibility is subject to the double condition that the proposed division does not have the effect of removing the project from the application of article
L. 181-1 and that it is consistent with environmental issues. The environmental authorizations issued in this context are, where applicable, supplemented in order to take into account the cumulative environmental impacts on the scale of the project.
L. 181-8 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The petitioner provides a file whose elements, when they are common to all requests for environmental authorization, are fixed by the decree in the Council of State provided for by article
L. 181-32 and which includes in particular the impact study provided for in Article III
L. 122-1 or an environmental impact study when the project is not subject to environmental assessment. A decree specifies the other documents and specific information to be attached to the file according to the legislation to which the project is subject, as well as the terms of its instruction. p.120 L. 181-9 LAW n°2023-973 of October 23, 2023 - art. 4 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The examination of the environmental authorization application, after it has been deemed complete and regular by the administrative authority, takes place in two phases: 1° An examination and consultation phase; 2° A decision phase. However, the competent administrative authority may reject the request during the examination and consultation phase when this shows that authorization cannot be granted in the current state of the file or project. This is particularly the case when the environmental authorization or, where applicable, the planning authorization necessary for the completion of the project, appears manifestly incapable of being issued having regard to the land use defined by the local plan. town planning or the document in place thereof or the municipal map in force at the time of the instruction, unless a procedure for revising, modifying or making the town planning document compatible with the effect of allowing this issuance is engaged.
L. 181-10 LAW n°2023-973 of October 23, 2023 - art. 4 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I. - The public consultation is carried out according to the terms set out in article
L. 181-10 -1. However, in the case provided for in the third paragraph of III of article
L. 122-1 -1, it is carried out according to the methods provided for in article
L. 123-19. When the examination of the planning authorization relating to the same project requires the implementation of one of the methods of public participation mentioned in 1° to 3° of the article
L. 123-1 -A and this procedure has not yet been carried out, the consultation provided for in article
L. 181-10-1 takes its place. When a public inquiry must also be carried out prior to a decision other than a planning authorization, necessary for the completion of the project, and this inquiry has not yet been carried out, public consultation is organized. in accordance with Chapter III of Title II of this book by a single public inquiry, unless an exemption is requested by the petitioner and granted, when this procedure is likely to promote the successful completion of the project, by the administrative authority competent to issue the authorization environmental. By way of derogation from article
L. 123-6, this single public inquiry is opened and organized by the administrative authority mentioned in the third paragraph of this I. Its duration cannot be less than one month. The investigation file includes all the elements required for the issuance of the environmental authorization. II.-The competent administrative authority requests an opinion from the local authorities and their groups interested in the project. When the project is subject to environmental assessment in application of II of article
L. 122-1, this referral replaces the transmission imposed by V of this article. It also replaces the consultation carried out, where applicable, within the framework of III of article L. 122-1

cidTexte=JORFTEXT000048242288 https://legifrance.gouv.fr/codes/article_lc/LEGIARTI000048244482/2024-05-14 https://www.courdecassation.fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20181-10-1%20 %22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environment%22&judilibre_juridiction=cc&op=Search+on+judilibre https://www.courdeca ssation.fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20181-10-1%20%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=ca&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://legifrance.gouv.fr /search/cetat?tab_selection=cetat&searchField=ALL&query=%22article%20L181-10-1%20%22%20du%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&page=1&init=true&dateDecision= https://juricaf.org/ research/article%20L.%20181-10-1%20%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VIII: Administrative or enforcement procedures several substitutes who can immediately replace the investigating commissioner or the members of the commission of inquiry in the event of their incapacity. As soon as the file is considered complete and regular and the investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry is appointed, the administrative authority organizes a public consultation in accordance with the procedures provided for in II to V of this article, unless the request has already been submitted. been rejected under the conditions provided for in the fourth paragraph of the article e
L. 181-9. II. - The consultation mentioned in the second paragraph of I of this article is intended to ensure the information and participation of the public as well as the taking into account of the interests of third parties during the development of the decision. The observations and proposals received during the consultation period are taken into consideration by the project owner and by the competent authority to make the decision. The public is informed of the opening of the consultation in accordance with the terms provided for in II of article
L. 123-19. The duration of the consultation is three months or, when the opinion of the environmental authority is required, one month longer than the deadline given to the latter to deliver its opinion. The consultation file is compiled and made available to the public under the conditions provided for in the same II. The impact study, when required, is made available to the public no later than the opening of the consultation. The opinions collected by the administration on the request or the indication of a lack of opinion resulting from the expiration of the deadlines are made available to the public without delay as they are issued. III. - The consultation is conducted by the investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry so as to allow the public to have complete information on the project and to participate effectively in the decision-making process. To this end: 1° Within fifteen days from the start of the consultation, the investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry organizes an opening public meeting with the participation of the petitioner; 2° The public may send its observations and proposals, during the duration of the consultation, by electronic mail, by postal as well as by any other method specified in the notice of opening of the consultation; 3° Observations and proposals transmitted electronically are accessible on a designated website under conditions established by regulation; 4° Any responses from the petitioner to opinions posted online as well as to observations and proposals from the public are transmitted and published under the same conditions, including when these responses were formulated during a public meeting; 5° In the last fifteen days of the public consultation, the investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry organizes a public closing meeting, with the participation of the petitioner. The investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry collects observations from stakeholders until the consultation closes. The responses provided by the petitioner at the latest at the closing meeting of the consultation are deemed to be part of the application file, as are any subsequent modifications to the project, provided that they do not modify its economy. general. IV. - The investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry renders its report and its reasoned conclusions automatically administrative procedure, after consultation with the petitioner and within three weeks from the close of the public consultation. The report sets out the main elements relating to the project collected during the public consultation and includes a summary of the public's observations and proposals as well as the petitioner's responses. The report and the reasoned conclusions are made public. Receipt of this report and these reasoned conclusions or the expiry of the three-week period ends the examination and consultation phase and opens the decision phase. The decision cannot be adopted before the expiry of a period allowing consideration of the observations and proposals made during the consultation and of the petitioner's responses. p.122 Environmental Code Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VIII: Administrative procedures V. - The petitioner assumes the costs relating to the public consultation, in particular those relating to the various measures of publicity of the consultation and to compensation of the investigating commissioner or the commission of inquiry, under the conditions provided for in Chapter III of Title II of this book 
L. 181-11 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The rules of procedure and consultation relating to environmental authorization replace the rules of procedure and consultation provided for by the other books of this code and by other legislation, insofar as they relate to the issuance of the decisions mentioned in article L. 181-2
L. 181-12 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The environmental authorization sets out the requirements necessary to comply with the provisions of articles L. 181-3
L. 181-4. These requirements relate, without prejudice to the provisions of article
L. 122-1 -1, on the measures and means to be implemented during the implementation of the project, during its operation, at the time of its cessation and after this, in particular the avoidance and reduction measures and compensation for significant negative effects on the environment and health. They may also relate to equipment and installations already operated and activities already carried out by the petitioner or authorized for his benefit when their connection makes them necessary for the activities, installations, works and works subject to authorization or whose proximity is likely to significantly modify the dangers or disadvantages. Section 4: Project implementation
L. 181-13 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When the project presents dangers or disadvantages of particular importance, the competent administrative authority may, both during the examination of an environmental authorization application and subsequent to its delivery, request a third party expertise in order to carry out the analysis of elements of the file requiring specific verifications. This third-party expertise is carried out by an external organization chosen in agreement with the administration by the petitioner and at the latter's expense.
L. 181-14 Ordinance No. 2020-7 of January 6, 2020 - art. 12 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Any substantial modification of activities, installations, works or works which fall under the environmental authorization is subject to the issuance of a new authorization, whether it occurs before the project is carried out or during its implementation or operation. . Apart from substantial modifications, any notable modification occurring in the same circumstances is brought to the attention of the administrative authority competent to issue the environmental authorization under the conditions defined by the decree provided for in article
L. 181-32. The competent administrative authority may impose any additional requirements necessary to comply with the provisions of Articles L. 181-3
L. 181-4 on the occasion of these modifications, but also at any time if it appears that compliance with these provisions is not ensured by the execution of the requirements previously issued. p.123 Environmental Code http://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichTexte.do?cidTexte=JORFTEXT000033926976 https://legifrance.gouv.fr/codes/article_lc/LEGIARTI000033928591/2024-05-14 https:// www.courdecassation.fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20181-11%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=cc&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://www.courdecassation.fr/recherche -judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20181-11%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=ca&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://legifrance.gouv.fr/search/cetat?tab_selection=cetat&searchField= ALL&query=%22article%20L181-11%22%20du%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&page=1&init=true&dateDecision= https://juricaf.org/recherche/article%20L.%20181-11%20% 22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC http://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichTexte.do?cidTexte=JORFTEXT000033926976 https://legifrance.gouv.fr/codes/article_lc/LEGIARTI000033928595/ 2024-05-2 p.m. ttps://www.courdecassation.fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20181-12%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=cc&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://www.courdecassation .fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20181-12%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=ca&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://legifrance.gouv.fr/search/cetat? L. 181-15 Ordinance No. 2020-7 of January 6, 2020 - art. 12 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The change of beneficiary of the environmental authorization is subject to a declaration to the competent administrative authority or to an authorization thereof, in the cases and conditions set by the decree provided for in article
L. 181-32. The extension and renewal of an environmental authorization are subject to the issuance of a new authorization if they involve a substantial modification of the authorized project or in the event of a substantial change in the factual and legal circumstances leading to the issuance of the authorization. the initial authorization. Otherwise, the provisions of the last paragraph of article L. 181-14
L. 181-15 -1 LAW n°2020-1525 of December 7, 2020 - art. 56 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When one or more third parties wish, with the agreement of the holder(s) of an environmental authorization, to benefit from a partial transfer thereof, they make a request to the competent administrative authority. When the latter considers that the modification is not substantial, that the partial transfer can be carried out without harming the interests mentioned in articles L. 181-3
L. 181-4, that the conditions provided for in articles L. 181-26
L. 181-27 are, where applicable, combined and it is possible to identify the measures relating to each, in particular to ensure the application of article
L. 181-12, it delivers to each applicant and the initial holder a separate environmental authorization. Section 5: Control and sanctions
L. 181-16 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.-For the application of this chapter, administrative controls are exercised and administrative police measures are taken under the conditions set out in Chapter I of Title VII of this book as well as, for activities, installations, works and works falling under 3° of the article
L. 181-1 of this code, in Title VII of Book I of the Mining Code, and by the legislation to which these controls and these measures relate. II.-For the application of this chapter, offenses are investigated, noted and punished under the conditions set out in section 2 of chapter II of title VII of this book and by the legislation which provides for them. III.-In addition to the officers and agents of the judicial police, civil servants and agents specially authorized under the provisions of Section 1 of Chapter II of Title VII of this book and others are authorized to investigate and report the offenses mentioned in II. legislation as well as, for activities, installations, structures and works falling under 3° of article
L. 181-1 of this code, in Title VII of Book I of the Mining Code, the competent agents in mining police matters.
L. 181-17 LAW n°2023-973 of October 23, 2023 - art. 4 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The decisions taken on the basis of the fourth paragraph of the article
L. 181-9 and the decisions mentioned in articles L. 181-12
L. 181-15 are subject to litigation of full jurisdiction. The author of the appeal is required, under penalty of inadmissibility, to notify his appeal to the author of the decision and the beneficiary of the decision. The conditions of application of this paragraph are specified by decree of the Council of State. When the right to appeal against one of the decisions mentioned in the first paragraph of this article is implemented under conditions which reflect abusive behavior on the part of the applicant and which cause harm to the beneficiary of the authorization, that -he may request, by a separate brief, the administrative judge seized of the appeal to order the author of the appeal to pay him damages. The application may be presented for the first time on appeal. p.124 Environmental Code http://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichTexte.do?cidTexte=JORFTEXT000039780349 https://juricaf.org/recherche/article%20L.%20181-17%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC Legislative part - Book I: Common provisions - Title VIII: Administrative procedures
L. 181-18 LAW n°2023-175 of March 10, 2023 - art. 23 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.-The administrative judge who, having received conclusions directed against an environmental authorization, considers, after having noted that the other means are unfounded, even after the completion of the work: 1° That a defect only affects 'a phase of the examination of the environmental authorization application, or part of this authorization, limits to this phase or this part the scope of the cancellation that it pronounces and asks the competent administrative authority to resume the investigation at the phase or on the part which was tainted by irregularity; 2° That a defect leading to the illegality of this act is likely to be regularized, i.e. suspending the ruling, after having invited the parties to present their observations, until the expiration of the deadline it sets for this regularization. If a regularization measure is notified to the judge within this period, the judge rules after inviting the parties to present their observations. The judge's refusal to grant a request for partial annulment or suspension of proceedings is justified. II.-In the event of cancellation or suspension of proceedings affecting only part of the environmental authorization, the judge determines whether there is reason to suspend the execution of the parts of the authorization which are not vitiated. Section 6: Provisions specific to certain categories of projects Subsection 1: Installations, structures, works and activities likely to have impacts on water and aquatic environments

L. 181-23 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When installations, works, works or activities are definitively stopped, the operator or, failing that, the owner returns the site to a state such that no harm can be done to the protected interests mentioned in article
L. 181-3. He informs the competent administrative authority of the cessation of the activity and the measures taken. This authority may at any time impose requirements for the restoration of the site, without prejudice to the application of articles L. 163-1 L. 163-9
L. 163-11 of the mining code. The provisions provided for in this article are not applicable to the installations, structures and works of hydraulic companies licensed under Title II of Book V of the Energy Code.
L. 181-23 -1 LAW n°2020-1525 of December 7, 2020 - art. 48 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf When activities, installations, structures or works arise from a civil emergency situation as mentioned in article
L. 122-3 -4, requests for environmental authorization are processed within the time limits and according to the procedures set by decree of the Council of State. In this case, the petitioner requests from the competent administrative authority the information provided for in 1° of article
L. 181-5 allowing it to prepare its project and the file for its authorization request. Subsection 2: Facilities classified for environmental protection
L. 181-24 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The provisions of this subsection are applicable to projects falling under 2° of Article L. 181-1
L. 181-25 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The applicant provides a hazard study which specifies the risks to which the installation may expose, directly or indirectly, the interests mentioned in article
L. 511-1 in the event of an accident, whether the cause is internal or external to the installation. The content of the hazard study must be related to the importance of the risks generated by the installation. Where necessary, this study gives rise to a risk analysis which takes into account the probability of occurrence, the kinetics and the severity of potential accidents according to a methodology which it explains. It defines and justifies measures to reduce the probability and effects of these accidents.
L. 181-26 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The issuance of the authorization may be subject in particular to the distance of the installations from homes, buildings usually occupied by third parties, establishments open to the public, watercourses, communication routes, water catchments , areas frequented by the public, leisure areas, areas of particular natural interest or having a particularly sensitive character or areas intended for habitation by planning documents enforceable against third parties.

L. 181-28 Ordinance No. 2017-80 of January 26, 2017 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf For installations whose operation for an unlimited period would create unacceptable dangers or inconveniences for the interests mentioned in article
L. 511-1, due to increasing use of the soil or subsoil, the authorization sets the maximum duration of the exploitation or the exploitation phase concerned and, where applicable, the maximum volume of products stored or extracted, as well as the conditions for redevelopment, monitoring and surveillance of the site at the end of operation. Subsection 3: Offshore renewable energy production facilities
L. 181-28 -1 LAW n°2018-727 of August 10, 2018 - art. 58 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.-For offshore renewable energy production installations and their connection works to public electricity networks, the following provisions are applicable: 1° All or part of the impact study may be carried out and implemented made available to project owners by the Minister responsible for energy; 2° The following authorizations set, where applicable, variable characteristics for these installation projects within the limits of which these projects are authorized to evolve subsequent to the issuance of the authorization: a) The single authorization provided for in article 20 of Order No. 2016-1687 of December 8, 2016 relating to maritime areas falling under the sovereignty or jurisdiction of the French Republic; b) The concession for use of the maritime public domain provided for in article
L. 2124-3 of the general code of property of public entities; c) The environmental authorization provided for in this chapter; d) The authorization to operate provided for in Section 2 of Chapter I of Title I of Book III of the Energy Code; 3° The requirements of the aforementioned authorizations, relating in particular to avoidance, reduction and compensation measures, are established taking into account the non-variable characteristics and the variable characteristics within which the installation project is authorized to evolve; 4° The petitioner informs the administrative authority competent to issue the aforementioned authorizations of the characteristics of the project as it is carried out and the associated avoidance, reduction and compensation measures. II.-I is not applicable to offshore renewable energy production installations and their connection works to public electricity networks for which the petitioner has regularly submitted an authorization request mentioned in 2° of I up to six months after the publication of Law No. 2018-727 of August 10, 2018 for a State serving a trusted society. Subsection 4: Installations for producing electricity from mechanical wind energy
L. 181-28 -2 LAW n°2021-1104 of August 22, 2021 - art. 82 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Without prejudice to the provisions of article
L. 181-5, the promoter of a project concerning an installation for the production of electricity from the mechanical energy of the wind addresses the mayors of the municipality concerned and the neighboring municipalities, at least one month before the submission of the application for environmental authorization, the non-technical summary of the impact study provided for in e of 2° of II of the article
L. 122-3. Within one month of sending the non-technical summary and after deliberation by the municipal council, the mayor of the municipality where the project is implemented sends his observations on the project to the project leader. In the absence of a reaction after this deadline, the mayor is deemed to have given up sending his observations. p.127 Environmental Code L. 181-1. Subsection 5: Mine research and exploitation work
L. 181-28 -3 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The provisions of this subsection are applicable to projects falling under 3° of article L. 181-1
L. 181-28 -4 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.-The authorization can only be issued if the measures it includes are compatible with the provisions resulting from the mining title, in particular, where it exists, with the environmental measures of the specifications mentioned in III of the article
L. 114-3 of the mining code. II.-The issuance of the authorization may be subject, in particular, to the distance of the activities from homes and buildings usually occupied by third parties, establishments open to the public, watercourses, roads communications, water catchments, areas frequented by the public, leisure areas and areas of particular natural interest or having a particularly sensitive character or intended for habitation under planning documents enforceable against third parties as well as 'with the consent of the owner provided for in articles L. 153-1 L. 153-2
L. 181-28 -5 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The article
L. 181-25 of this code is applicable to projects falling under 3° of article
L. 181-1 of the same code, with the exception of research and exploitation of geothermal deposits. The hazard study takes into account the interests mentioned in article L. 161-1
L. 181-28 -6 Ordinance No. 2022-534 of April 13, 2022 - art. 1 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf For research or exploitation of liquid or gaseous hydrocarbons in the territorial sea, in the exclusive economic zone or on the continental shelf, the authorization mentioned in 3° of the article
L. 181-1 of this code is subject to the evaluation and acceptance by the competent administrative authority of the report on major dangers as well as the description of the independent verification program established for the installations listed in article 162-6-2 of the mining code, without prejudice to the liability of the applicant. For the work mentioned in the previous paragraph, the report on major dangers replaces the danger study provided for in article
L. 181-28 -5. Worker representatives are consulted during the preparation of the report on major hazards. The report on major hazards is subject to a thorough review by the operator at least every five years or sooner, when the competent administrative authority requires it.

If the regularization occurs within the set deadline, it is notified to the judge, who rules after inviting the parties to present their observations. p.131 Environmental Code http://legifrance.gouv.fr/affichTexte.do?cidTexte=JORFTEXT000039355955 https://legifrance.gouv.fr/codes/article_lc/LEGIARTI000039360349/2024-05-14 https:// www.courdecassation.fr/recherche-judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20191-1%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=cc&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://www.courdecassation.fr/recherche -judilibre?search_api_fulltext=%22L%20191-1%22+%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&judilibre_juridiction=ca&op=Rechercher+sur+judilibre https://legifrance.gouv.fr/search/cetat?tab_selection=cetat&searchField= ALL&query=%22article%20L191-1%22%20du%20%22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22&page=1&init=true&dateDecision= https://juricaf.org/recherche/article%20L.%20191-1%20% 22Code%20de%20l%27environnement%22/facet_pays%3AFrance?tri=DESC Legislative part - Book II: Physical environments - Title I: Water and aquatic and marine environments Book II: Physical environments Title I: Water and aquatic and marine environments
L. 210-1 Ordinance No. 2022-1611 of December 22, 2022 - art. 3 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Water is part of the common heritage of the nation. Its protection, its development and the development of the usable resource, while respecting natural balances, are of general interest. Respect for natural balances implies the preservation and, where appropriate, the restoration of the natural functionalities of aquatic ecosystems, whether superficial or underground, of which wetlands are a part, and of marine ecosystems, as well as their interactions. These functionalities are essential to the recovery of biodiversity, adaptation to climate change as well as the mitigation of its effects and contribute to the fight against pollution. As such, aquatic ecosystems and marine ecosystems constitute essential elements of the Nation's heritage. Within the framework of laws and regulations as well as previously established rights, the use of water belongs to everyone and each natural person has the right to access drinking water, according to the terms and for the essential uses mentioned in the article
L. 1321-1 A of the public health code, under conditions economically acceptable to all. Costs related to water use, including costs to the environment and the resources themselves, are borne by users taking into account social, environmental and economic consequences as well as geographical and climatic conditions. Chapter I: General regime and resource management
L. 211-1 LAW n°2020-105 of February 10, 2020 - art. 69 (V) Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf I.-The provisions of Chapters I to VII of this Title aim at balanced and sustainable management of water resources; this management takes into account the necessary adaptations to climate change and aims to ensure: 1° The prevention of flooding and the preservation of aquatic ecosystems, sites and wetlands; Wetland means land, exploited or not, usually flooded or saturated with fresh, salt or brackish water permanently or temporarily, or whose vegetation, when it exists, is dominated by hygrophilous plants for at least one part of the year; 2° The protection of waters and the fight against any pollution by spills, flows, discharges, direct or indirect deposits of materials of any nature and more generally by any fact likely to cause or increase the degradation of waters by modifying their physical characteristics , chemical, biological or bacteriological, whether surface water, groundwater or sea water within the limits of territorial waters; 3° Restoration of the quality of these waters and their regeneration; 4° The development, mobilization, creation and protection of the resource in water ; 5° The valorization of water as an economic resource and, in particular, for the development of the production of electricity from renewable sources as well as the distribution of this resource; II.-Balanced management must make it possible, as a priority, to satisfy the requirements of health, public safety, civil security and the supply of drinking water to the population. It must also make it possible to satisfy or reconcile, during different uses, activities or works, the requirements: 1° Of the biological life of the receiving environment, and especially of the fish and shellfish fauna; 2° Conservation and free flow of water and protection against flooding; 3° Agriculture, fisheries and marine cultures, freshwater fishing, industry, energy production, in particular to ensure the security of the electrical system, transport, tourism, the protection of sites, leisure activities and water sports as well as all other legally exercised human activities. III.-Balanced management of water resources does not hinder the preservation of hydraulic heritage, in particular hydraulic mills and their outbuildings, structures designed to use the hydraulic power of rivers and lakes and seas, protected either as historic monuments, surroundings or remarkable heritage sites in application of Book VI of the Heritage Code, or in application of article
L. 151-19 of the town planning code.
L. 211-1 -1 Law n°2005-157 of February 23, 2005 - art. 127 () JORF February 24, 2005 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The preservation and sustainable management of wetlands defined in article
L. 211-1 are of general interest. National, regional and local policies for the development of rural areas and the allocation of public aid take into account the particular difficulties of conservation, exploitation and sustainable management of wetlands and their contribution to policies for the preservation of biological diversity. , landscape, water resources management and flood prevention in particular through adapted agriculture, pastoralism, forestry, hunting, fishing and tourism. To this end, the State and its public establishments, the regions, the departments, the municipalities and their groups ensure, each in their area of ​​competence, the coherence of the various public policies in these territories. For the application of the X of the article
L. 212-1, the State ensures that this consistency is taken into account in water development and management plans.

L. 211-4 Ordinance 2000-914 2000-09-18 JORF September 21, 2000 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf Water quality standards may be set by the competent state authorities in certain areas of seas and oceans, salt ponds, estuaries and deltas up to the water saline limit, depending on their contribution to exploitation and development activities of the biological resources of these areas. These activities may be regulated or prohibited based on these quality standards. This provision also applies to the marketing of plant or animal products derived from these waters and intended for human consumption.
L. 211-5 Ordinance 2000-914 2000-09-18 JORF September 21, 2000 Legif. Plan Jp.C.Cass. Jp.Call Jp.Admin. Juricaf The prefect and mayor concerned must be informed, as soon as possible by any person who has knowledge of it, of any incident or accident presenting a danger to civil safety, quality, circulation or conservation of water. The person causing the incident or accident and the operator or, if there is no operator, the owner are required, as soon as they become aware of it, to take or cause to be taken all possible measures to put an end to the cause of danger or damage to the aquatic environment, evaluate the c onconsequences of the incident or accident and remedy them. The prefect may prescribe to the persons mentioned above the measures to be taken to put an end to the damage observed or to limit its seriousness and, in particular, the analyzes to be carried out. In the event of a deficiency, and if there is a risk of pollution or destruction of the natural environment, or for public health and the supply of drinking water, the prefect may take or have the necessary measures carried out at the expense and risk. responsible people. The prefect and the mayor concerned inform the populations by all appropriate means of the circumstances of the incident or accident, its foreseeable effects and the measures are taken to remedy this. Public fire and rescue service agents have access to private property to put an end to the causes of danger or damage to the aquatic environment and prevent or limit the consequences of the incident or accident. Without prejudice to compensation for other damages suffered, public legal entities involved materially or financially are entitled to reimbursement, by the person(s) responsible for the incident or accident, of the costs incurred by they. As such, they can become a civil party before the criminal courts seized of proceedings following the incident or accident.

I bis.-The municipalities are responsible for the management of aquatic environments and flood prevention. This skill includes the missions defined in 1°, 2°, 5° and 8° of I. For this purpose, they may use the procedure provided for in the same I. I ter. - When the state of surface water or groundwater presents health and environmental issues justifying coordinated management of the different hydrographic sub-basins of the region , the regional council may be assigned all or part of the coordination and consultation missions in the field of management and protection of water resources and aquatic environments mentioned in 12° of I of this article, by decree , at his request and after opinion of the territorial conference of public action mentioned in article e
L. 1111-9 -1 of the general code of local authorities. The region exercises these powers in coordination with the basin committee, without prejudice to the skills of other communities, their groups and mixed unions, and without prejudice to the missions of the legal entities under public law to which the local water commission has entrusted its secretariat, as well as, where applicable, the studies and analyzes necessary for the development of the water development and management plan and the monitoring of its implementation. I quater.-By derogation from the rule according to which an open mixed union mentioned in article
L. 5721-2 of the general code of local authorities cannot join another open mixed union, such a union exercising one of the missions mentioned in 1°, 2°, 5° and 8° of I of this article may, until December 31, 2020, under these skills and with the agreement of the basin coordinator prefect, join another open mixed union. From January 1, 2021, this possibility is reserved for public water development and management establishments mentioned in II of the article
L. 213-12 of this code and to mixed unions integrating the status of public water development and management establishment in application of 2° of VII bis of the same article
L. 213-12, who wish to join public territorial basin establishments mentioned in I of said article
L. 213-12. II.-The study, execution and exploitation of said works may be granted in particular to mixed economy companies. The dealers are entitled to receive the price of the participations provided for in article
L. 151-36 of the rural and maritime fishing code. III.-A single public inquiry is carried out in accordance with Chapter III of Title II of Book I of this Code under Article
L. 151-37 of the rural and maritime fishing code, article
